exec
/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
path = Path('roomsim/roomsim.cpp')
source = path.read_text()
start = source.index('struct SimulationState {')
end = source.index('// ============================================================================\\n// Hash for Verification', start)
source = source[:start] + r'''// Contiguous receiver rows keep each floating-point sum on one rank, in the
// original emitter order. Geometry is replicated; the quadratic matrices and
// complete radiosity traces are distributed.
struct SimulationState {
    MPI_Comm comm;
    int rank, numRanks;
    size_t numTriangles = 0;
    size_t numTimesteps = 0;
    size_t firstTriangle = 0;
    size_t localTriangles = 0;
    size_t sourceIndex = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = ZERO;
    int minDelay = INT_MAX;
    int maxDelay = 0;
    uint64_t randomDraws = 0;
    uint64_t nonZeroKij = 0;

    std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
    std::vector<val_t> areas;
    // Local receiver rows, with all emitters in global order.
    std::vector<val_t> kij;
    std::vector<uint8_t> tau;       // Radius 10 and wave speed 0.5 give delays <= 40.
    // Triangle-major local traces make cross-correlation contiguous.
    std::vector<val_t> radB;
    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Global on rank zero, local elsewhere.
    Octree octree;

    explicit SimulationState(MPI_Comm communicator) : comm(communicator) {
        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
        MPI_Comm_size(comm, &numRanks);
    }

    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t i) const { return i * numTimesteps + t; }
};

// ============================================================================
// Initialization
// ============================================================================

void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);
    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
    state.reflectivity = reflectivity;

    state.counts.resize(state.numRanks);
    state.displacements.resize(state.numRanks);
    size_t first = 0;
    for (int rank = 0; rank < state.numRanks; ++rank) {
        size_t count = state.numTriangles / state.numRanks +
                       (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < state.numTriangles % state.numRanks);
        state.counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count);
        state.displacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(first);
        first += count;
    }
    state.firstTriangle = state.displacements[state.rank];
    state.localTriangles = state.counts[state.rank];

    if (state.rank == 0) {
        printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);
        printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");
    }
    state.octree.build(state.triangles);

    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
    }

    state.kij.resize(state.localTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.tau.resize(state.localTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.localTriangles, ZERO);
    state.distances.resize(state.rank == 0 ? state.numTriangles : state.localTriangles, ZERO);
}

// ============================================================================
// Precomputation Phase
// ============================================================================

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");

    // Every non-culled pair consumes exactly four float draws per ray, even
    // when a ray is blocked. Exscan locates each row block in the original
    // seed-42 stream; discard skips engine draws without computing geometry.
    uint64_t precedingDraws = 0;
    MPI_Exscan(&state.randomDraws, &precedingDraws, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, state.comm);
    if (state.rank == 0) precedingDraws = 0;
    RandomGenerator rng(42);
    rng.discard(precedingDraws);

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localTriangles; ++i) {
        const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (globalI == j) continue;
            val_t kij = computeKij(globalI, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = kij;
            if (kij > EPSILON) ++state.nonZeroKij;
            if (kij > ZERO) {
                const int delay = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];
                state.minDelay = std::min(state.minDelay, delay);
                state.maxDelay = std::max(state.maxDelay, delay);
            }
        }
    }
    int minDelay = state.minDelay, maxDelay = state.maxDelay;
    MPI_Allreduce(&minDelay, &state.minDelay, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, state.comm);
    MPI_Allreduce(&maxDelay, &state.maxDelay, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, state.comm);
    if (state.minDelay == INT_MAX) state.minDelay = 1;
    if (state.rank == 0) {
        printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
    }
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localTriangles; ++i) {
        const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (globalI == j) continue;
            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = static_cast<uint8_t>(
                computeTau(state.triangles[globalI], state.triangles[j]));
            if ("'!(state.triangles[globalI].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f)) {
                state.randomDraws += 4 * NUM_RAYS;
            }
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...'"\\n\");
    if (state.numTimesteps == 0) return;

    // Precompute the time-invariant transfer weights in place, retaining the
    // original form-factor count for validation. This avoids two dense matrices.
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            val_t& kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
            if (kij > ZERO) kij = std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE);
        }
    }

    // No receiver needs a value less than minDelay steps old. Compute that
    // many steps between exchanges, amortizing MPI latency. Only maxDelay
    // past steps are needed for propagation; full traces stay on their owners.
    const size_t batchSize = std::min(state.numTimesteps, static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay));
    const size_t historySteps = std::min(state.numTimesteps,
        static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + batchSize);
    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    const size_t localN = state.localTriangles;
    std::vector<val_t> history(historySteps * n, ZERO);
    std::vector<val_t> localBatch(batchSize * localN, ZERO);
    std::vector<val_t> received(batchSize * n, ZERO);
    std::vector<int> batchCounts(state.numRanks), batchDisplacements(state.numRanks);

    for (size_t firstTime = 0; firstTime < state.numTimesteps; firstTime += batchSize) {
        const size_t steps = std::min(batchSize, state.numTimesteps - firstTime);
        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
            const size_t t = firstTime + q;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < localN; ++i) {
                const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;
                val_t sumB = ZERO;
                const val_t* weights = state.kij.data() + i * n;
                const uint8_t* delays = state.tau.data() + i * n;
                for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                    if (globalI == j) continue;
                    const size_t delay = delays[j];
                    if (t < delay) continue;
                    const val_t weight = weights[j];
                    if (weight <= ZERO) continue;
                    const val_t radJ = history[((t - delay) % historySteps) * n + j];
                    if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
                    sumB += weight * radJ;
                }
                const val_t emission = globalI == state.sourceIndex && t < state.numTimesteps / 2
                                     ? ONE : ZERO;
                const val_t value = state.reflectivity * sumB + emission;
                state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = value;
                localBatch[q * localN + i] = value;
            }
        }

        // Received blocks are rank-major, with time-major rows inside each.
        for (int rank = 0; rank < state.numRanks; ++rank) {
            batchCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(steps) * state.counts[rank];
            batchDisplacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(steps) * state.displacements[rank];
        }
        MPI_Allgatherv(localBatch.data(), static_cast<int>(steps * localN), MPI_FLOAT,
                       received.data(), batchCounts.data(), batchDisplacements.data(),
                       MPI_FLOAT, state.comm);
        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
            val_t* row = history.data() + ((firstTime + q) % historySteps) * n;
            for (int rank = 0; rank < state.numRanks; ++rank) {
                const size_t count = state.counts[rank];
                std::copy_n(received.data() + batchDisplacements[rank] + q * count,
                            count, row + state.displacements[rank]);
            }
            if (state.rank == 0 && ((firstTime + q + 1) % 10 == 0 ||
                                   firstTime + q + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {
                printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", firstTime + q + 1, state.numTimesteps);
            }
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");
    std::vector<val_t> source(state.numTimesteps);
    int sourceRank = 0;
    while (sourceRank + 1 < state.numRanks &&
           state.sourceIndex >= static_cast<size_t>(state.displacements[sourceRank + 1])) {
        ++sourceRank;
    }
    if (state.rank == sourceRank && state.numTimesteps "'!= 0) {
        const size_t localSource = state.sourceIndex - state.firstTriangle;
        std::copy_n(state.radB.data() + localSource * state.numTimesteps,
                    state.numTimesteps, source.data());
    }
    MPI_Bcast(source.data(), static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps), MPI_FLOAT, sourceRank, state.comm);

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;
            const val_t* trace = state.radB.data() + i * state.numTimesteps;
            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                sum += source[tt - t] * trace[tt];
            }
            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;
                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
            }
        }
        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
    }

    // The root'"'s row block starts at zero, so its results are already in place.
    MPI_Gatherv(state.rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : state.distances.data(),
                static_cast<int>(state.localTriangles), MPI_FLOAT,
                state.distances.data(), state.counts.data(), state.displacements.data(),
                MPI_FLOAT, 0, state.comm);
}

// ============================================================================
// Validation
// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
    uint64_t localEnergy = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {
                ++localEnergy;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    uint64_t receivedEnergy = 0, nonZeroKij = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localEnergy, &receivedEnergy, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0, state.comm);
    MPI_Reduce(&state.nonZeroKij, &nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0, state.comm);

    int valid = 1;
    if (state.rank == 0) {
        printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");
        val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
        val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
        val_t sumDist = ZERO;
        size_t nonZeroCount = 0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
            const val_t d = state.distances[i];
            if (d < 0 || "'!std::isfinite(d)) {
                printf("  ERROR: Invalid distance at triangle %zu: %f'"\\n\", i, d);
                valid = 0;
            }
            minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
            maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
            sumDist += d;
            if (d > EPSILON) ++nonZeroCount;
        }
        printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);
        printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));
        printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %zu/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);
        const val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];
        if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {
            printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);
        }
        printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %llu/%zu\\n\",
               static_cast<unsigned long long>(receivedEnergy), state.numTriangles);
        printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %llu/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",
               static_cast<unsigned long long>(nonZeroKij), state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,
               100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));
        if (receivedEnergy == 0) {
            printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");
            valid = 0;
        }
        if (nonZeroKij == 0) {
            printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");
            valid = 0;
        }
        if (valid) printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");
    }
    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, state.comm);
    return valid "'!= 0;
}

long phaseMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm) {
    double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double maximum = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &maximum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
    return static_cast<long>(maximum * 1000.0);
}

int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, int worldRank, int worldSize) {
    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;

            validate = true;

            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            if (worldRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;

            if (worldRank == 0) {

                printUsage(argv[0]);

    for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) numTriangles *= 4;

    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD,

                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);
    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"\\n\");

    computeTimeDelays(state);
    computeFormFactors(state);
    const long preDuration = phaseMilliseconds(startPre, comm);
    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Precomputation time: %ld ms\\n\\n\", preDuration);

    const double startSim = MPI_Wtime();

        printf(\"\\nPerformance:\\n\");
        printf(\"  Triangles: %zu\\n\", n);
        printf(\"  Timesteps: %zu\\n\", t);

    }

    return status;
}

    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);

    }

// ============================================================================
// Tau (time delay) Computation
// ============================================================================

int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
}

// ============================================================================
// Room Response Simulation State
// ============================================================================

// Contiguous receiver rows keep each floating-point sum on one rank, in the
// original emitter order. Geometry is replicated; the quadratic matrices and
// complete radiosity traces are distributed.
struct SimulationState {
    MPI_Comm comm;
    int rank, numRanks;
    size_t numTriangles = 0;
    size_t numTimesteps = 0;
    size_t firstTriangle = 0;
    size_t localTriangles = 0;
    size_t sourceIndex = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = ZERO;
    int minDelay = INT_MAX;
    int maxDelay = 0;
    uint64_t randomDraws = 0;
    uint64_t nonZeroKij = 0;

    std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
    std::vector<val_t> areas;
    // Local receiver rows, with all emitters in global order.
    std::vector<val_t> kij;
    std::vector<uint8_t> tau;       // Radius 10 and wave speed 0.5 give delays <= 40.
    // Triangle-major local traces make cross-correlation contiguous.
    std::vector<val_t> radB;
    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Global on rank zero, local elsewhere.
    Octree octree;

    explicit SimulationState(MPI_Comm communicator) : comm(communicator) {
        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
        MPI_Comm_size(comm, &numRanks);
    }

    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t i) const { return i * numTimesteps + t; }
};

// ============================================================================
// Initialization
// ============================================================================

void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);
    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
    state.reflectivity = reflectivity;

    state.counts.resize(state.numRanks);
    state.displacements.resize(state.numRanks);
    size_t first = 0;
    for (int rank = 0; rank < state.numRanks; ++rank) {
        size_t count = state.numTriangles / state.numRanks +
                       (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < state.numTriangles % state.numRanks);
        state.counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count);
        state.displacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(first);
        first += count;
    }
    state.firstTriangle = state.displacements[state.rank];
    state.localTriangles = state.counts[state.rank];

    if (state.rank == 0) {
        printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
        printf("Building octree...\n");
    }
    state.octree.build(state.triangles);

    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
    }

    state.kij.resize(state.localTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.tau.resize(state.localTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.localTriangles, ZERO);
    state.distances.resize(state.rank == 0 ? state.numTriangles : state.localTriangles, ZERO);
}

// ============================================================================
// Precomputation Phase
// ============================================================================

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");

    // Every non-culled pair consumes exactly four float draws per ray, even
    // when a ray is blocked. Exscan locates each row block in the original
    // seed-42 stream; discard skips engine draws without computing geometry.
    uint64_t precedingDraws = 0;
    MPI_Exscan(&state.randomDraws, &precedingDraws, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, state.comm);
    if (state.rank == 0) precedingDraws = 0;
    RandomGenerator rng(42);
    rng.discard(precedingDraws);

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localTriangles; ++i) {
        const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (globalI == j) continue;
            val_t kij = computeKij(globalI, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = kij;
            if (kij > EPSILON) ++state.nonZeroKij;
            if (kij > ZERO) {
                const int delay = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];
                state.minDelay = std::min(state.minDelay, delay);
                state.maxDelay = std::max(state.maxDelay, delay);
            }
        }
    }
    int minDelay = state.minDelay, maxDelay = state.maxDelay;
    MPI_Allreduce(&minDelay, &state.minDelay, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, state.comm);
    MPI_Allreduce(&maxDelay, &state.maxDelay, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, state.comm);
    if (state.minDelay == INT_MAX) state.minDelay = 1;
    if (state.rank == 0) {
        printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
    }
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localTriangles; ++i) {
        const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (globalI == j) continue;
            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = static_cast<uint8_t>(
                computeTau(state.triangles[globalI], state.triangles[j]));
            if (!(state.triangles[globalI].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f)) {
                state.randomDraws += 4 * NUM_RAYS;
            }
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
    if (state.numTimesteps == 0) return;

    // Precompute the time-invariant transfer weights in place, retaining the
    // original form-factor count for validation. This avoids two dense matrices.
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            val_t& kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
            if (kij > ZERO) kij = std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE);
        }
    }

    // No receiver needs a value less than minDelay steps old. Compute that
    // many steps between exchanges, amortizing MPI latency. Only maxDelay
    // past steps are needed for propagation; full traces stay on their owners.
    const size_t batchSize = std::min(state.numTimesteps, static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay));
    const size_t historySteps = std::min(state.numTimesteps,
        static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + batchSize);
    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    const size_t localN = state.localTriangles;
    std::vector<val_t> history(historySteps * n, ZERO);
    std::vector<val_t> localBatch(batchSize * localN, ZERO);
    std::vector<val_t> received(batchSize * n, ZERO);
    std::vector<int> batchCounts(state.numRanks), batchDisplacements(state.numRanks);

    for (size_t firstTime = 0; firstTime < state.numTimesteps; firstTime += batchSize) {
        const size_t steps = std::min(batchSize, state.numTimesteps - firstTime);
        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
            const size_t t = firstTime + q;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < localN; ++i) {
                const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;
                val_t sumB = ZERO;
                const val_t* weights = state.kij.data() + i * n;
                const uint8_t* delays = state.tau.data() + i * n;
                for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                    if (globalI == j) continue;
                    const size_t delay = delays[j];
                    if (t < delay) continue;
                    const val_t weight = weights[j];
                    if (weight <= ZERO) continue;
                    const val_t radJ = history[((t - delay) % historySteps) * n + j];
                    if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
                    sumB += weight * radJ;
                }
                const val_t emission = globalI == state.sourceIndex && t < state.numTimesteps / 2
                                     ? ONE : ZERO;
                const val_t value = state.reflectivity * sumB + emission;
                state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = value;
                localBatch[q * localN + i] = value;
            }
        }

        // Received blocks are rank-major, with time-major rows inside each.
        for (int rank = 0; rank < state.numRanks; ++rank) {
            batchCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(steps) * state.counts[rank];
            batchDisplacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(steps) * state.displacements[rank];
        }
        MPI_Allgatherv(localBatch.data(), static_cast<int>(steps * localN), MPI_FLOAT,
                       received.data(), batchCounts.data(), batchDisplacements.data(),
                       MPI_FLOAT, state.comm);
        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
            val_t* row = history.data() + ((firstTime + q) % historySteps) * n;
            for (int rank = 0; rank < state.numRanks; ++rank) {
                const size_t count = state.counts[rank];
                std::copy_n(received.data() + batchDisplacements[rank] + q * count,
                            count, row + state.displacements[rank]);
            }
            if (state.rank == 0 && ((firstTime + q + 1) % 10 == 0 ||
                                   firstTime + q + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {
                printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", firstTime + q + 1, state.numTimesteps);
            }
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
    std::vector<val_t> source(state.numTimesteps);
    int sourceRank = 0;
    while (sourceRank + 1 < state.numRanks &&
           state.sourceIndex >= static_cast<size_t>(state.displacements[sourceRank + 1])) {
        ++sourceRank;
    }
    if (state.rank == sourceRank && state.numTimesteps != 0) {
        const size_t localSource = state.sourceIndex - state.firstTriangle;
        std::copy_n(state.radB.data() + localSource * state.numTimesteps,
                    state.numTimesteps, source.data());
    }
    MPI_Bcast(source.data(), static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps), MPI_FLOAT, sourceRank, state.comm);

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;
            const val_t* trace = state.radB.data() + i * state.numTimesteps;
            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                sum += source[tt - t] * trace[tt];
            }
            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;
                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
            }
        }
        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
    }

    // The root's row block starts at zero, so its results are already in place.
    MPI_Gatherv(state.rank == 0 ? MPI_IN_PLACE : state.distances.data(),
                static_cast<int>(state.localTriangles), MPI_FLOAT,
                state.distances.data(), state.counts.data(), state.displacements.data(),
                MPI_FLOAT, 0, state.comm);
}

// ============================================================================
// Validation
// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
    uint64_t localEnergy = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {
                ++localEnergy;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    uint64_t receivedEnergy = 0, nonZeroKij = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localEnergy, &receivedEnergy, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0, state.comm);
    MPI_Reduce(&state.nonZeroKij, &nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0, state.comm);

    int valid = 1;
    if (state.rank == 0) {
        printf("\nValidation:\n");
        val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
        val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
        val_t sumDist = ZERO;
        size_t nonZeroCount = 0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
            const val_t d = state.distances[i];
            if (d < 0 || !std::isfinite(d)) {
                printf("  ERROR: Invalid distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
                valid = 0;
            }
            minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
            maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
            sumDist += d;
            if (d > EPSILON) ++nonZeroCount;
        }
        printf("  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\n", minDist, maxDist);
        printf("  Average distance: %.4f\n", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));
        printf("  Non-zero distances: %zu/%zu\n", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);
        const val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];
        if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {
            printf("  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\n", srcDist);
        }
        printf("  Triangles receiving energy: %llu/%zu\n",
               static_cast<unsigned long long>(receivedEnergy), state.numTriangles);
        printf("  Non-zero form factors: %llu/%zu (%.2f%%)\n",
               static_cast<unsigned long long>(nonZeroKij), state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,
               100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));
        if (receivedEnergy == 0) {
            printf("  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\n");
            valid = 0;
        }
        if (nonZeroKij == 0) {
            printf("  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\n");
            valid = 0;
        }
        if (valid) printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
    }
    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, state.comm);
    return valid != 0;
}

// ============================================================================
// Hash for Verification
// ============================================================================

uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        uint32_t bits;
        std::memcpy(&bits, &state.distances[i], sizeof(bits));
        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(bits) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    }
    return hash;
}

// ============================================================================
// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
// ============================================================================

int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
    int subdivisions = 0;
    int triangles = 20;
    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
        subdivisions++;
        triangles *= 4;
    }
    return subdivisions;
}

// ============================================================================
// Main
// ============================================================================

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

// Report the slowest rank rather than the time of an arbitrary participant.
long phaseMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm) {
    double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double maximum = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &maximum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
    return static_cast<long>(maximum * 1000.0);
}
}

int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, int worldRank, int worldSize) {
    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (worldRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (worldRank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            return 1;
        }
    }
    if (timesteps < 0) {
        if (worldRank == 0) fprintf(stderr, "Number of timesteps must be non-negative\n");
        return 1;
    }

    const int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);
    size_t numTriangles = 20;
    for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) numTriangles *= 4;

    // Exclude ranks that cannot own a row from the high-frequency collectives.
    // All world ranks still participate in final status and MPI finalization.
    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD,
                   static_cast<size_t>(worldRank) < numTriangles ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED,
                   worldRank, &comm);
    if (comm == MPI_COMM_NULL) return 0;

    SimulationState state(comm);
    if (state.rank == 0) {
        printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
        printf("===================================\n");
        printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
        printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
        printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
        printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
        printf("MPI ranks: %d (active: %d)\n\n", worldSize, state.numRanks);
    }
    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("\n");

    MPI_Barrier(comm);
    const double startPre = MPI_Wtime();
    computeTimeDelays(state);
    computeFormFactors(state);
    const long preDuration = phaseMilliseconds(startPre, comm);
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n\n", preDuration);

    MPI_Barrier(comm);
    const double startSim = MPI_Wtime();
    runSimulation(state);
    const long simDuration = phaseMilliseconds(startSim, comm);
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n\n", simDuration);

    MPI_Barrier(comm);
    const double startDist = MPI_Wtime();
    computeDistances(state);
    const long distDuration = phaseMilliseconds(startDist, comm);

    uint64_t localMem = state.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t) + state.tau.size() * sizeof(uint8_t) +
                        state.radB.size() * sizeof(val_t);
    uint64_t maxMem = 0, totalMem = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localMem, &maxMem, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
    MPI_Reduce(&localMem, &totalMem, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0, comm);
    if (state.rank == 0) {
        printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n\n", distDuration);
        const long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
        printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);
        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
        const size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
        const double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n) * n;
        const double simOps = kijOps * t;
        const double distOps = static_cast<double>(n) * t * t;
        printf("\nPerformance:\n");
        printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
        printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
        printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
        printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
        printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
        printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);
        printf("  Matrix/trace memory per rank (maximum): %.2f MB\n", maxMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
        printf("  Matrix/trace memory (total): %.2f MB\n", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
        printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n", static_cast<unsigned long long>(computeHash(state)));
        if (printResults) {
            std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());
            print_results(distData, "Distances");
        }
    }

    const int status = validate && !validateResults(state) ? 1 : 0;
    MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
    return status;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
    int rank = 0, size = 0;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
    int status = 0;
    try {
        status = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, size);
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Finalize();
    return status;
}

        std::vector<int> counts = state.counts, displacements = state.displacements;

            counts[rank] *= state.numTriangles;

                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT, 0, comm);

        std::vector<int> counts = state.counts, displacements = state.displacements;
        for (int rank = 0; rank < state.numRanks; ++rank) {
            counts[rank] *= state.numTimesteps;
            displacements[rank] *= state.numTimesteps;
        }
        std::vector<val_t> traces(state.rank == 0 ? state.numTriangles * state.numTimesteps : 0);
        MPI_Gatherv(state.radB.data(), state.radB.size(), MPI_FLOAT, traces.data(),
                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT, 0, comm);

                }
            }
            print_results(ordered, \"Radiosity\");
        }
    }
    const long distDuration = phaseMilliseconds(startDist, comm);''')

]
def results(output):
    blocks = re.findall(r'=== RESULTS ===\\n(.*?)=== END RESULTS ===', output, re.S)
    return {re.search(r'Name: (.*)', block)[1]: block for block in blocks}

    args = ['-n', str(n), '-t', str(t), '-s', str(s), '-r', rho, '-o', '-v']
    reference = subprocess.run(['/tmp/roomsim-reference-check', *args], text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=90)
    distributed = subprocess.run(['mpirun', '--allow-run-as-root', '--oversubscribe', '-np', str(ranks), '/tmp/roomsim-mpi-check', *args], text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=90)
    assert reference.returncode == distributed.returncode, (args, reference.returncode, distributed.returncode, distributed.stderr)

    assert distributed.stdout.count('Name: Distances') == 1

The initial distance results match. Checks of the intermediate arrays caught a small rounding difference caused by compiler fusion after precomputing transfer weights. I’m keeping the original accumulation expression and checking the full form-factor, radiosity, and distance arrays.

            const size_t width = matrix == 0 ? state.numTriangles : state.numTimesteps;
            const auto& local = matrix == 0 ? state.kij : state.radB;

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
    if (state.numTimesteps == 0) return;

    // No receiver needs a value less than minDelay steps old. Compute that
    // many steps between exchanges, amortizing MPI latency. Only maxDelay
    // past steps are needed for propagation; full traces stay on their owners.
    const size_t batchSize = std::min(state.numTimesteps, static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay));
    const size_t historySteps = std::min(state.numTimesteps,
        static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + batchSize);
    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    const size_t localN = state.localTriangles;
    std::vector<val_t> history(historySteps * n, ZERO);
    std::vector<val_t> localBatch(batchSize * localN, ZERO);
    std::vector<val_t> received(batchSize * n, ZERO);
    std::vector<int> batchCounts(state.numRanks), batchDisplacements(state.numRanks);
    std::vector<const val_t*> delayedRows(static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + 1);

    for (size_t firstTime = 0; firstTime < state.numTimesteps; firstTime += batchSize) {
        const size_t steps = std::min(batchSize, state.numTimesteps - firstTime);
        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
            const size_t t = firstTime + q;
            // Resolve ring-buffer offsets once per timestep, outside the
            // quadratic loop. The emitter loop then needs no integer division.
            for (size_t delay = 0; delay < delayedRows.size() && delay <= t; ++delay) {
                delayedRows[delay] = history.data() + ((t - delay) % historySteps) * n;
            }
            for (size_t i = 0; i < localN; ++i) {
                const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;
                val_t sumB = ZERO;
                const val_t* formFactors = state.kij.data() + i * n;
                const uint8_t* delays = state.tau.data() + i * n;
                if (t >= static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay)) {
                    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                        const size_t delay = delays[j];
                        if (t < delay) continue;
                        const val_t kij = formFactors[j];
                        if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
                        const val_t radJ = delayedRows[delay][j];
                        if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
                        // Keep the original expression and emitter order,
                        // including its intermediate floating-point rounding.
                        sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
                    }
                }
                const val_t emission = globalI == state.sourceIndex && t < state.numTimesteps / 2
                                     ? ONE : ZERO;
                const val_t value = state.reflectivity * sumB + emission;
                state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = value;
                localBatch[q * localN + i] = value;
            }
        }

        // Received blocks are rank-major, with time-major rows inside each.
        // The final block is already in the owners' traces and needs no exchange.
        if (firstTime + steps < state.numTimesteps) {
            for (int rank = 0; rank < state.numRanks; ++rank) {
                batchCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(steps) * state.counts[rank];
                batchDisplacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(steps) * state.displacements[rank];
            }
            MPI_Allgatherv(localBatch.data(), static_cast<int>(steps * localN), MPI_FLOAT,
                           received.data(), batchCounts.data(), batchDisplacements.data(),
                           MPI_FLOAT, state.comm);
            for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
                val_t* row = history.data() + ((firstTime + q) % historySteps) * n;
                for (int rank = 0; rank < state.numRanks; ++rank) {
                    const size_t count = state.counts[rank];
                    std::copy_n(received.data() + batchDisplacements[rank] + q * count,
                                count, row + state.displacements[rank]);
                }
            }
        }
        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
            if (state.rank == 0 && ((firstTime + q + 1) % 10 == 0 ||
                                   firstTime + q + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {
                printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", firstTime + q + 1, state.numTimesteps);
            }
        }
    }
}

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
    std::vector<val_t> source(state.numTimesteps);
    int sourceRank = 0;
    while (sourceRank + 1 < state.numRanks &&
           state.sourceIndex >= static_cast<size_t>(state.displacements[sourceRank + 1])) {
        ++sourceRank;
    }

        const size_t localSource = state.sourceIndex - state.firstTriangle;
        std::copy_n(state.radB.data() + localSource * state.numTimesteps,
                    state.numTimesteps, source.data());
    }
    MPI_Bcast(source.data(), static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps), MPI_FLOAT, sourceRank, state.comm);

            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                sum += source[tt - t] * trace[tt];
            }
            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;

import re
import subprocess
cases = [
    (1, 20, 0, 0, '.8'),
    (3, 20, 1, 19, '.8'),
    (7, 20, 2, 19, '.8'),
    (3, 20, 17, 33, '.8'),
    (4, 20, 93, -1, '1'),
    (3, 80, 89, 79, '.8'),
    (4, 80, 80, 27, '0'),
    (7, 80, 120, 80, '.2'),
    (4, 320, 97, 319, '.8'),
    (24, 20, 87, 19, '.8'),
]
def results(output):
    blocks = re.findall(r'=== RESULTS ===\\n(.*?)=== END RESULTS ===', output, re.S)
    return {re.search(r'Name: (.*)', block)[1]: block for block in blocks}
for ranks, n, t, s, rho in cases:
    args = ['-n', str(n), '-t', str(t), '-s', str(s), '-r', rho, '-o', '-v']
    reference = subprocess.run(['/tmp/roomsim-reference-check', *args], text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=90)
    distributed = subprocess.run(['mpirun', '--allow-run-as-root', '--oversubscribe', '-np', str(ranks), '/tmp/roomsim-mpi-check-final', *args], text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=90)
    assert reference.returncode == distributed.returncode, (args, reference.returncode, distributed.returncode, distributed.stderr)
    assert results(reference.stdout) == results(distributed.stdout), (args, results(reference.stdout), results(distributed.stdout))
    assert distributed.stdout.count('Name: Distances') == 1
    for prefix in ('Distance range:', 'Average distance:', 'Non-zero distances:', 'Triangles receiving energy:', 'Non-zero form factors:'):
        assert re.search(re.escape(prefix)+r'.*', reference.stdout)[0] == re.search(re.escape(prefix)+r'.*', distributed.stdout)[0], prefix
    print(f'PASS: ranks={ranks}, triangles={n}, timesteps={t}, source={s}, reflectivity={rho}; exact Kij, radiosity, distance hashes and statistics; exit={distributed.returncode}', flush=True)

 void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {

+    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");

+    // No receiver needs a value less than minDelay steps old. Compute that
+    // many steps between exchanges, amortizing MPI latency. Only maxDelay
+    // past steps are needed for propagation; full traces stay on their owners.
+    const size_t batchSize = std::min(state.numTimesteps, static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay));
+    const size_t historySteps = std::min(state.numTimesteps,
+        static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + batchSize);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+    const size_t localN = state.localTriangles;
+    std::vector<val_t> history(historySteps * n, ZERO);
+    std::vector<val_t> localBatch(batchSize * localN, ZERO);
+    std::vector<val_t> received(batchSize * n, ZERO);
+    std::vector<int> batchCounts(state.numRanks), batchDisplacements(state.numRanks);
+    std::vector<const val_t*> delayedRows(static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + 1);
 
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
-                if (i == j) continue;
+    for (size_t firstTime = 0; firstTime < state.numTimesteps; firstTime += batchSize) {
+        const size_t steps = std::min(batchSize, state.numTimesteps - firstTime);
+        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
+            const size_t t = firstTime + q;
+            // Resolve ring-buffer offsets once per timestep, outside the
+            // quadratic loop. The emitter loop then needs no integer division.
+            for (size_t delay = 0; delay < delayedRows.size() && delay <= t; ++delay) {
+                delayedRows[delay] = history.data() + ((t - delay) % historySteps) * n;
+            }
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < localN; ++i) {
+                const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;
+                val_t sumB = ZERO;
+                const val_t* formFactors = state.kij.data() + i * n;
+                const uint8_t* delays = state.tau.data() + i * n;
+                if (t >= static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay)) {
+                    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                        const size_t delay = delays[j];
+                        if (t < delay) continue;
+                        const val_t kij = formFactors[j];
+                        if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
+                        const val_t radJ = delayedRows[delay][j];
+                        if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
+                        // Keep the original expression and emitter order,
+                        // including its intermediate floating-point rounding.
+                        sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
+                    }

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import re
import subprocess
cases = [
    (1, 20, 0, 0, '.8'),
    (3, 20, 1, 19, '.8'),
    (7, 20, 2, 19, '.8'),
    (3, 20, 17, 33, '.8'),
    (4, 20, 93, -1, '1'),
    (3, 80, 89, 79, '.8'),
    (4, 80, 80, 27, '0'),
    (7, 80, 120, 80, '.2'),
    (4, 320, 97, 319, '.8'),
    (24, 20, 87, 19, '.8'),
]
def results(output):
    blocks = re.findall(r'=== RESULTS ===\\n(.*?)=== END RESULTS ===', output, re.S)
    return {re.search(r'Name: (.*)', block)[1]: block for block in blocks}
for ranks, n, t, s, rho in cases:
    args = ['-n', str(n), '-t', str(t), '-s', str(s), '-r', rho, '-o', '-v']
    reference = subprocess.run(['/tmp/roomsim-reference-check', *args], text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=90)
    distributed = subprocess.run(['mpirun', '--allow-run-as-root', '--oversubscribe', '-np', str(ranks), '/tmp/roomsim-mpi-check-final', *args], text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=90)
    assert reference.returncode == distributed.returncode, (args, reference.returncode, distributed.returncode, distributed.stderr)
    assert results(reference.stdout) == results(distributed.stdout), (args, results(reference.stdout), results(distributed.stdout))
    assert distributed.stdout.count('Name: Distances') == 1
    for prefix in ('Distance range:', 'Average distance:', 'Non-zero distances:', 'Triangles receiving energy:', 'Non-zero form factors:'):
        ref_line = re.search(re.escape(prefix)+r'.*', reference.stdout)
        if ref_line is not None:
            assert ref_line[0] == re.search(re.escape(prefix)+r'.*', distributed.stdout)[0], prefix
    print(f'PASS: ranks={ranks}, triangles={n}, timesteps={t}, source={s}, reflectivity={rho}; exact Kij, radiosity, distance hashes and statistics; exit={distributed.returncode}', flush=True)
PY" in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/roomsim_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_mpi_r4

 void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
+    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");

+    // No receiver needs a value less than minDelay steps old. Compute that
+    // many steps between exchanges, amortizing MPI latency. Only maxDelay
+    // past steps are needed for propagation; full traces stay on their owners.
+    const size_t batchSize = std::min(state.numTimesteps, static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay));
+    const size_t historySteps = std::min(state.numTimesteps,
+        static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + batchSize);

+        const size_t steps = std::min(batchSize, state.numTimesteps - firstTime);
+        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
+            const size_t t = firstTime + q;
+            // Resolve ring-buffer offsets once per timestep, outside the
+            // quadratic loop. The emitter loop then needs no integer division.
+            for (size_t delay = 0; delay < delayedRows.size() && delay <= t; ++delay) {
+                delayedRows[delay] = history.data() + ((t - delay) % historySteps) * n;
+            }
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < localN; ++i) {
+                const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;

+                const uint8_t* delays = state.tau.data() + i * n;
+                if (t >= static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay)) {
+                    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                        const size_t delay = delays[j];
+                        if (t < delay) continue;
+                        const val_t kij = formFactors[j];
+                        if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
+                        const val_t radJ = delayedRows[delay][j];
+                        if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
+                        // Keep the original expression and emitter order,
+                        // including its intermediate floating-point rounding.
+                        sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
+                    }
+                }
+                const val_t emission = globalI == state.sourceIndex && t < state.numTimesteps / 2
+                                     ? ONE : ZERO;
+                const val_t value = state.reflectivity * sumB + emission;
+                state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = value;

-                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
-                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
+        // Received blocks are rank-major, with time-major rows inside each.
+        // The final block is already in the owners' traces and needs no exchange.
+        if (firstTime + steps < state.numTimesteps) {

+                for (int rank = 0; rank < state.numRanks; ++rank) {

+                    std::copy_n(received.data() + batchDisplacements[rank] + q * count,
+                                count, row + state.displacements[rank]);
+                }
             }
-
-            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission

+    // No receiver needs a value less than minDelay steps old. Compute that
+    // many steps between exchanges, amortizing MPI latency. Only maxDelay
+    // past steps are needed for propagation; full traces stay on their owners.
+    const size_t batchSize = std::min(state.numTimesteps, static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay));
+    const size_t historySteps = std::min(state.numTimesteps,
+        static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + batchSize);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;

+    std::vector<val_t> history(historySteps * n, ZERO);

+    for (size_t firstTime = 0; firstTime < state.numTimesteps; firstTime += batchSize) {
+        const size_t steps = std::min(batchSize, state.numTimesteps - firstTime);
+        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
+            const size_t t = firstTime + q;
+            // Resolve ring-buffer offsets once per timestep, outside the
+            // quadratic loop. The emitter loop then needs no integer division.
+            for (size_t delay = 0; delay < delayedRows.size() && delay <= t; ++delay) {
+                delayedRows[delay] = history.data() + ((t - delay) % historySteps) * n;
+            }
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < localN; ++i) {

+                val_t sumB = ZERO;

+                const uint8_t* delays = state.tau.data() + i * n;
+                if (t >= static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay)) {

+                        const size_t delay = delays[j];
+                        if (t < delay) continue;

 void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {

+    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");

+    // No receiver needs a value less than minDelay steps old. Compute that
+    // many steps between exchanges, amortizing MPI latency. Only maxDelay
+    // past steps are needed for propagation; full traces stay on their owners.
+    const size_t batchSize = std::min(state.numTimesteps, static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay));
+    const size_t historySteps = std::min(state.numTimesteps,
+        static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + batchSize);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+    const size_t localN = state.localTriangles;
+    std::vector<val_t> history(historySteps * n, ZERO);
+    std::vector<val_t> localBatch(batchSize * localN, ZERO);

+    std::vector<int> batchCounts(state.numRanks), batchDisplacements(state.numRanks);
+    std::vector<const val_t*> delayedRows(static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + 1);

-                if (i == j) continue;
+    for (size_t firstTime = 0; firstTime < state.numTimesteps; firstTime += batchSize) {
+        const size_t steps = std::min(batchSize, state.numTimesteps - firstTime);
+        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
+            const size_t t = firstTime + q;
+            // Resolve ring-buffer offsets once per timestep, outside the
+            // quadratic loop. The emitter loop then needs no integer division.
+            for (size_t delay = 0; delay < delayedRows.size() && delay <= t; ++delay) {
+                delayedRows[delay] = history.data() + ((t - delay) % historySteps) * n;
+            }
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < localN; ++i) {
+                const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;
+                val_t sumB = ZERO;
+                const val_t* formFactors = state.kij.data() + i * n;
+                const uint8_t* delays = state.tau.data() + i * n;
+                if (t >= static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay)) {
+                    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                        const size_t delay = delays[j];
+                        if (t < delay) continue;
+                        const val_t kij = formFactors[j];
+                        if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
+                        const val_t radJ = delayedRows[delay][j];
+                        if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;

 // ============================================================================
 
 void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
+    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
+    if (state.numTimesteps == 0) return;

-            val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    // No receiver needs a value less than minDelay steps old. Compute that
+    // many steps between exchanges, amortizing MPI latency. Only maxDelay
+    // past steps are needed for propagation; full traces stay on their owners.
+    const size_t batchSize = std::min(state.numTimesteps, static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay));
+    const size_t historySteps = std::min(state.numTimesteps,
+        static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + batchSize);
+    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+    const size_t localN = state.localTriangles;
+    std::vector<val_t> history(historySteps * n, ZERO);
+    std::vector<val_t> localBatch(batchSize * localN, ZERO);
+    std::vector<val_t> received(batchSize * n, ZERO);
+    std::vector<int> batchCounts(state.numRanks), batchDisplacements(state.numRanks);
+    std::vector<const val_t*> delayedRows(static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + 1);
 
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
-                if (i == j) continue;
+    for (size_t firstTime = 0; firstTime < state.numTimesteps; firstTime += batchSize) {
+        const size_t steps = std::min(batchSize, state.numTimesteps - firstTime);
+        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
+            const size_t t = firstTime + q;
+            // Resolve ring-buffer offsets once per timestep, outside the
+            // quadratic loop. The emitter loop then needs no integer division.
+            for (size_t delay = 0; delay < delayedRows.size() && delay <= t; ++delay) {
+                delayedRows[delay] = history.data() + ((t - delay) % historySteps) * n;
+            }
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < localN; ++i) {
+                const size_t globalI = state.firstTriangle + i;
+                val_t sumB = ZERO;
+                const val_t* formFactors = state.kij.data() + i * n;
+                const uint8_t* delays = state.tau.data() + i * n;
+                if (t >= static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay)) {
+                    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                        const size_t delay = delays[j];
+                        if (t < delay) continue;
+                        const val_t kij = formFactors[j];

+    // many steps between exchanges, amortizing MPI latency. Only maxDelay
+    // past steps are needed for propagation; full traces stay on their owners.
+    const size_t batchSize = std::min(state.numTimesteps, static_cast<size_t>(state.minDelay));
+    const size_t historySteps = std::min(state.numTimesteps,
+        static_cast<size_t>(state.maxDelay) + batchSize);

+        for (size_t q = 0; q < steps; ++q) {
+            const size_t t = firstTime + q;
+            // Resolve ring-buffer offsets once per timestep, outside the
+            // quadratic loop. The emitter loop then needs no integer division.
+            for (size_t delay = 0; delay < delayedRows.size() && delay <= t; ++delay) {

+                        // Keep the original expression and emitter order,
+                        // including its intermediate floating-point rounding.
+                        sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;

codex
Implemented unconditional MPI, updating only `roomsim.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt`. The executable remains `roomsim`.

Distributed matrix rows and radiosity traces, with batched communication and preserved random streams and arithmetic.

Exact array hashes matched at 1, 3, 4, 7, 8, and 24 ranks. Build and UBSan checks passed. The local 1280-triangle benchmark improved from **27.11 s to 3.47 s** with 8 ranks.

Run: `mpirun -np 4 <build-dir>/roomsim -v -o`

Distributed matrix rows and radiosity traces, with batched communication and preserved random streams and arithmetic.

Exact array hashes matched at 1, 3, 4, 7, 8, and 24 ranks. Build and UBSan checks passed. The local 1280-triangle benchmark improved from **27.11 s to 3.47 s** with 8 ranks.
